CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

邓鹤轩Hexuan Deng[1 2 ] 柯晓鹏Xiaopeng Ke[1 ] 李一尘Yichen Li[2 3 ] 胡瑞娜Ruina Hu[2 4 ] 德里克·黄Derek F. Wong[5] 王悦Yue Wang[2][+] 刘学波Xuebo Liu[1][+] 黄德豪Dehao Huang[2] 张敏Min Zhang[1]

摘要Abstract

尽管人工智能审阅者发展迅速，但评估此类系统仍然具有挑战性：指标更倾向于与人类审阅重叠，而不是正确性。然而，由于人工审查通常只涵盖突出问题的一部分，有时还包含错误，因此它们作为黄金参考并不可靠。为了解决这个问题，我们构建了特定于类别的基准子集，并在缺少相应的人工评论时跳过评估以加强Despite the rapid development of AI reviewers, evaluating such systems remains challenging: metrics favor overlap with human reviews over correctness. However, since human reviews often cover only a subset of salient issues and sometimes contain mistakes, they are unreliable as gold references. To address this, we build categoryspecific benchmark subsets and skip evaluation when the corresponding human reviews are missing to strengthenCo完整性。我们还利用审稿人-作者-元审稿讨论作为专家注释，并相应地过滤不可靠的审稿，以加强mpleteness. We also leverage reviewer–author–meta-review discussions as expert annotations and filter unreliable reviews accordingly to strengthenCo正确性。最后，我们介绍 CoCoReviewBench，它整理了 ICLR 和 NeurIPS 的 3,900 篇论文，以便对 AI 审稿人进行可靠且细粒度的评估。分析表明，人工智能审稿人的正确性仍然有限，并且容易产生幻觉，并强调推理模型是更有效的审稿人，这为改进人工智能审稿人提供了进一步的方向。基准和模型可在 https://github.com/hexuandeng/CoCoReviewBench 获取。rrectness. Finally, we introduce CoCoReviewBench, which curates 3,900 papers from ICLR and NeurIPS to enable reliable and fine-grained evaluation of AI reviewers. Analysis shows that AI reviewers remain limited in correctness and are prone to hallucinations, and highlights reasoning models as more effective reviewers, motivating further directions for improving AI reviewers. Benchmarks and models are available at https://github.c om/hexuandeng/CoCoReviewBench.

一、简介1. Introduction

同行评审是科学进步的基石（Hillard & Baber，2021）。然而，随着投稿量的迅速激增，审稿人承受的压力越来越大，导致Peer review is a cornerstone of scientific progress (Hillard & Baber, 2021). However, with the rapid surge in submission volume, reviewers are bearing increasing pressure, leading

图 1.Figure 1.CoCoReviewBench 概述。为了解决不完整性问题，我们构建了每个类别的子集，其中仅包含具有该类别评论的论文。为了解决不正确的问题，我们合并了审稿人和作者关于同一点的讨论。当存在明确的冲突时，我们使用元审查作为最终决定的高级裁决信号。Overview of CoCoReviewBench. To tackle incompleteness, we build per-category subsets which contain only papers with reviews in that category. To address incorrectness, we merge discussions about the same point across the reviewers and authors. When explicit conflicts exist, we use the meta-review as a highlevel adjudication signal for the final decision.

审稿质量不可避免地下降（Kim et al., 2025；Beecher & Wang, 2025；Morley et al., 2025）。为了解决这个问题，大型语言模型（LLM）具有成为缓解​​这些问题的强大助手的巨大潜力（Mann et al., 2025；Thakkar et al., 2025），并且还刺激了构建人工智能审稿系统的巨大努力（Liu & Shah, 2023；Jin et al., 2024；Weng et al., 2025）。to an inevitable decline in review quality (Kim et al., 2025; Beecher & Wang, 2025; Morley et al., 2025). To address this, Large Language Models (LLMs) hold significant potential to become powerful assistants that can alleviate these problems (Mann et al., 2025; Thakkar et al., 2025), and also spur substantial efforts to build AI reviewer systems (Liu & Shah, 2023; Jin et al., 2024; Weng et al., 2025).

因此，评估人工智能审稿人变得至关重要。一系列工作直接采用LLM作为法官（Zheng et al., 2023）范式，而不使用人类意见作为参考（Xu et al., 2025；Zhang & Abernethy, 2025）。然而，法学硕士可能缺乏足够的领域专业知识，并且可能表现出偏见，导致分数夸大而没有进步（Dorner et al., 2025; Krumdick et al., 2025）。与此同时，更多的作品直接使用人类评论作为黄金参考，并评估与参考的相似性，而不是通过基于规则的匹配或法学硕士作为法官来评估正确性，从而避免了专家裁决的需要并减轻偏见（D’Arcy et al., 2024; Chang et al., 2025; Shin et al., 2025）。Therefore, evaluating AI reviewers becomes crucial. A line of work directly adopts the LLM-as-a-judge (Zheng et al., 2023) paradigm without using human opinions as references (Xu et al., 2025; Zhang & Abernethy, 2025). However, LLMs may lack sufficient domain expertise, and can exhibit biases that inflate scores without improvement (Dorner et al., 2025; Krumdick et al., 2025). Meanwhile, more works directly use human reviews as gold references and evaluate similarity to the reference rather than correctness via rulebased matching or LLM-as-a-judge, thereby avoiding the need for expert adjudication and mitigating bias (D’Arcy et al., 2024; Chang et al., 2025; Shin et al., 2025).

1

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

然而，同行评审的性质使其不适合作为黄金参考。最关键的问题是完整性。现有的同行评审通常只突出最突出的要点（Logullo 等人，2025 年；Rethlefsen 等人，2025 年）。因此，当人工智能评论与人类评论不重叠时，它不一定是错误的，但可能反映了人类评论中的缺失点（Shin et al., 2025；Matsubara, 2025）。此外，人类审稿人可能不一致，有时甚至不正确，并且可能因提交量的快速增长而进一步加剧（Meyerson 等人，2025 年；Bergstrom & Gross，2025 年）。However, the nature of peer review makes it unsuitable as a gold reference. The most critical issue is completeness. Existing peer reviews often only highlight the most salient points (Logullo et al., 2025; Rethlefsen et al., 2025). Therefore, when an AI review does not overlap with the human review, it is not necessarily incorrect, but may reflect missing points in the human review (Shin et al., 2025; Matsubara, 2025). In addition, human reviewers can be inconsistent and occasionally incorrect, and may be further exacerbated by the rapidly growing submission volume (Meyerson et al., 2025; Bergstrom & Gross, 2025).

为了解决这些问题，我们提出了 CoCoReviewBench，它增强了To address these issues, we propose CoCoReviewBench, which strengthens both theco完整性mpleteness和andco正确性rrectness参考人类评论的数量，如图 1 所示。为了解决不完整性问题，我们构建了一个包含 23 个子类别的全面的二级评论分类法。我们汇总所有审阅者的评论并构建每个类别的基准子集。当人工评论不包含子类别的评论时，我们会跳过对该子类别的评估，避免因不完整而导致的错误处罚。此外，为了解决不正确的问题，我们将作者、其他审稿人之间的讨论以及元审稿​​视为专家注释，而不是依赖可能缺乏专业知识的法学硕士法官。当存在冲突时，至少有一方是不正确的（Goldberg et al., 2023; Kargaran et al., 2025），我们使用元审查作为最终决定的高层裁决信号。最后，我们构建了来自 ICLR 和 NeurIPS 的 3,900 篇论文的基准，为 AI 审稿人提供了细粒度且可靠的分析。我们的贡献是：of the reference human reviews, as shown in Figure 1. To tackle incompleteness, we construct a comprehensive twolevel taxonomy of reviews with 23 subcategories. We aggregate comments from all reviewers and build per-category benchmark subsets. When the human review does not contain comments from a subcategory, we skip evaluation for that subcategory, avoiding incorrect penalization caused by incompleteness. Further, to address incorrectness, rather than relying on an LLM judge that may lack expertise, we treat discussions among the authors, other reviewers, and the meta-review, as expert annotations. When conflicts exist, at least one party is incorrect (Goldberg et al., 2023; Kargaran et al., 2025), and we use the meta-review as a high-level adjudication signal for the final decision. Finally, we construct a benchmark of 3,900 papers from ICLR and NeurIPS, providing a fine-grained and reliable analysis of AI reviewers. Our contributions are:

  • 我们揭示了人类评审中的不完整性和不正确性，这可能会影响人工智能评审员的评估和培训。We reveal incompleteness and incorrectness in human reviews, which can potentially affect the evaluation and training of AI reviewers.

  • 我们提出了 CoCoReviewBench，它可以进行类别级别的评估并检测错误的参考评论，以减轻人类参考的局限性。We propose CoCoReviewBench, which enables categorylevel evaluation and detects erroneous reference comments to mitigate the limitations of human references.

  • 分析表明，法学硕士在正确性方面落后于人类，容易产生幻觉，并且推理模型更好地证实了他们的评论。Analysis shows that LLMs lag behind humans in correctness, are prone to hallucinations, and that reasoning models better substantiate their reviews.

2.相关工作2. Related Work

人工智能审稿人。AI Reviewers.人工智能审稿人可以协助和提高人类审稿质量（Liang et al., 2024；Thakkar et al., 2025），并且可以生成与人类撰写的审稿相当的审稿（Weng et al., 2025）。然而，这引起了对偏见（Zhu et al., 2025a）、幻觉（Zhuang et al., 2025）和新颖性识别（Shin et al., 2025）的担忧。为了解决这个问题，一项工作是构建模拟人工审核工作流程的代理审核系统。高等人。 （2024）通过方面提示生成分解评论写作AI reviewers can assist and improve human review quality (Liang et al., 2024; Thakkar et al., 2025) and can produce reviews comparable to those written by humans (Weng et al., 2025). However, this raises concerns about bias (Zhu et al., 2025a), hallucination (Zhuang et al., 2025), and novelty recognition (Shin et al., 2025). To address this, one line of work builds agentic review systems that simulate the human reviewing workflow. Gao et al. (2024) decompose review writing via aspect-prompt genera-

化，朱等人。 (2025b) 构建多阶段、证据驱动的分析。达西等人。 （2024）和高等人。 (2025b) 使用多代理角色专业化和聚合，Taechoyotin 等人。 （2024）通过多模态检索扩展了这一点。然而，这些管道繁重的系统很难利用现有的同行评审语料库进行端到端优化，这限制了性能。tion, and Zhu et al. (2025b) construct multi-stage, evidencedriven analysis. D’Arcy et al. (2024) and Gao et al. (2025b) use multi-agent role specialization and aggregation, and Taechoyotin et al. (2024) extend this with multimodal retrieval. However, these pipeline-heavy systems are difficult to optimize end-to-end with existing peer review corpora, which limits performance.

另一项工作重点是利用大规模同行评审语料库。 PeerRead（Kang 等人，2018）首先将提交的内容与决策和专家撰写的评论配对。此外，袁等人。 (2022) 提供方面注释，MReD (Shen et al., 2022) 添加元评论作为目标，SEA (Yu et al., 2024) 将多个评论标准化为统一目标。 NLPeer（Dycke 等人，2023）提供了具有统一表示的更加多样化的语料库，而 Tan 等人。 （2024）和张等人。 (2025) 增强反驳痕迹以创建对话式语料库。在这些资源的基础上，在同行评审语料库上使用指令调整（Idahl & Ahmadi，2025；Weng 等人，2025）或强化学习（Taechoyotin & Acuna，2025；Zeng 等人，2025）来增强 AI 审稿人的能力。然而，这些方法将人类评论视为基本事实，并继承了人类的主观性和偶尔的错误（Cortes & Lawrence，2021；Mann 等人，2025）。Another line of work focuses on utilizing large-scale peerreview corpora. PeerRead (Kang et al., 2018) first pairs submissions with decisions and expert-written reviews. Further, Yuan et al. (2022) provide aspect annotations, MReD (Shen et al., 2022) adds meta-reviews as targets, and SEA (Yu et al., 2024) standardizes multiple reviews into unified targets. NLPeer (Dycke et al., 2023) offers a more diverse corpus with unified representations, while Tan et al. (2024) and Zhang et al. (2025) augment rebuttal traces to create a dialogue-style corpus. Building on these resources, instruction tuning (Idahl & Ahmadi, 2025; Weng et al., 2025) or reinforcement learning (Taechoyotin & Acuna, 2025; Zeng et al., 2025) is used on peer review corpora to enhance AI reviewers. However, these approaches treat human reviews as ground truth and inherit human subjectivity and occasional errors (Cortes & Lawrence, 2021; Mann et al., 2025).

受此启发，我们构建了一个结构化的审阅基准，并表明人工智能审阅者与人类审阅者一样，会遇到正确性问题，甚至可能产生幻觉，这限制了他们的可靠性。Motivated by this, we construct a structured review benchmark and show that AI reviewers, like human reviewers, suffer from correctness issues and can even hallucinate, which limits their reliability.

AI 审稿人的评估。Evaluation of AI Reviewers.随着人工智能评审员的迅速发展，可靠的评估变得至关重要。早期评估将审稿视为预测问题，从接受预测（Kang et al., 2018）到使用 MSE 和 MAE 回归总体评分（Zhang et al., 2025；Weng et al., 2025）。然而，由于评审者之间存在巨大分歧，这些目标很难可靠地解释（Cortes & Lawrence，2021；Mann 等人，2025）。对于生成的评论文本，基于规则的重叠度量通常根据人类参考来报告，例如 BLEU（Papineni 等人，2002）、ROUGE（Lin，2004）和 BERTScore（Zhang 等人，2020），但这种硬匹配很大程度上奖励了表面相似性，并且不适合有效批评承认高度多样化表达的同行评审（Xu 等人，2025）。As AI reviewers rapidly evolve, reliable evaluation becomes essential. Early evaluation treated reviewing as a prediction problem, from acceptance prediction (Kang et al., 2018) to regressing overall ratings with MSE and MAE (Zhang et al., 2025; Weng et al., 2025). However, these targets are difficult to interpret reliably due to substantial disagreement between reviewers (Cortes & Lawrence, 2021; Mann et al., 2025). For generated review text, rule-based overlap metrics are often reported against human references, e.g., BLEU (Papineni et al., 2002), ROUGE (Lin, 2004), and BERTScore (Zhang et al., 2020), but such hard matching largely rewards surface similarity and is ill-suited to peer review where valid critiques admit highly diverse expressions (Xu et al., 2025).

为了超越重叠指标，研究采用了 LLM-asa-judge 范式，从而实现更灵活的评估（Deng 等人，2024；2025a；Idahl 和 Ahmadi，2025；Gao 等人，2025a）。进一步的工作使评估更具诊断性，在意见层面评估评论，而不是分配单一的总体分数（Sadallah 等人，2025）。其中一项工作不使用人类参考（Xu et al., 2025；Zhang & Abernethy, 2025），这是可扩展的，但可能会引入偏差，从而提高分数，而不会改善实质性批评（Dorner et al., 2025；Szymanski et al., 2025）。另一行是指通过匹配来进行人工评论To move beyond overlap metrics, studies adopt the LLM-asa-judge paradigm, enabling more flexible evaluation (Deng et al., 2024; 2025a; Idahl & Ahmadi, 2025; Gao et al., 2025a). Further work makes the assessment more diagnostic, evaluating the reviews at the opinion level rather than assigning a single overall score (Sadallah et al., 2025). One line of work does not use human references (Xu et al., 2025; Zhang & Abernethy, 2025), which is scalable but can introduce biases that inflate scores without improving substantive critique (Dorner et al., 2025; Szymanski et al., 2025). Another line refers to human reviews by matching

2

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

AI 与他们一起评论。达西等人。 Chang 等人 (2024) 使用 LLM 来执行生成评论和人工评论之间的一致性。 (2025)同时使用LLM和基于嵌入的相似性，Shin等人。 (2025) 使用标签。然而，人类的观点有时可能是错误的，没有人类提到的观点并不总是意味着该观点对论文无效（Cortes & Lawrence，2021；Mann et al.，2025）。AI reviews with them. D’Arcy et al. (2024) use LLM to perform alignment between generated and human reviews, Chang et al. (2025) use both LLM and embedding-based similarity, and Shin et al. (2025) use labels. However, human opinions can be occasionally wrong, and the absence of a human-mentioned point does not always imply that the point is invalid for the paper (Cortes & Lawrence, 2021; Mann et al., 2025).

为了解决这些问题，我们引入类别级别评估作为折衷方案：我们匹配同一类别内的评论，而不是个人意见。我们仅评估人类评论涵盖的类别，解决了使用人类评论作为参考的不完整性问题。To address these, we introduce category-level evaluation as a compromise: we match reviews within the same category, rather than an individual opinion. We evaluate only categories covered by human reviews, resolving the incompleteness issue of using human reviews as a reference.

3. 人类审稿人的错误3. The Fallibility of Human Reviewers

为了证明人工审核中不适合直接作为黄金参考的问题，我们对以下内容进行了全面分析：To demonstrate the issues in human reviewing that make it unsuitable to directly serve as a gold reference, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of3,900来自 NeurIPS (2021-2024) 和 ICLR (2017-2025) 的论文，用于构建 CoCoReviewBench。papers from NeurIPS (2021–2024) and ICLR (2017–2025), which are used for the construction of CoCoReviewBench.

3.1.人类审稿人的不完整性3.1. The Incompleteness of Human Reviewers

高质量的评估参考应该对提交的内容进行全面评估。为了衡量完整性，基于 NeurIPS 2025 审稿人指南和 ACL 滚动审稿评审表，我们构建了 5 个顶级类别，23 个子类别，涵盖质量、清晰度、重要性、原创性和政策，构建过程和定义详见附录 A。A high-quality reference for evaluation should provide a holistic evaluation of a submission. To measure completeness, based on the NeurIPS 2025 Reviewer Guidelines and the ACL Rolling Review Review Form, we build five toplevel categories with 23 subcategories covering Quality, Clarity, Significance, Originality, and Policy, with construction process and definitions detailed in Appendix A.

稀疏的单一审稿人覆盖范围。Sparse Single-Reviewer Coverage.我们使用第 4 节中详述的管道标记所有人工评论，结果如图 2 所示。审稿人的注意力高度稀疏：单个审稿人平均只覆盖 5 个顶级类别中的 3.03 个，以及 23 个子类别中的 5.10 个，这表明每个审稿人通常只对评估空间的一小部分进行评论。这种固有的稀疏性不仅在评估过程中产生不完整的参考，而且还给监督训练带来了巨大的挑战，因为模型可能有继承这种不完整的评估逻辑的风险。We label all human reviews with the pipeline detailed in §4, and results are shown in Figure 2. Reviewer attention is highly sparse: a single reviewer covers only 3.03 out of 5 top-level categories and 5.10 out of 23 subcategories on average, indicating that each reviewer typically comments on only a small fraction of the evaluation space. This inherent sparsity not only yields an incomplete reference during evaluation, but also presents a substantial challenge for supervised training, as models may risk inheriting this incomplete evaluation logic.

多审稿人受益于剩余的不完整性。Multi-Reviewer Gains with Residual Incompleteness.合并所有审稿人后，一篇论文平均涵盖 5 个类别中的 3.98 个类别和 23 个子类别中的 9.23 个子类别，与单个审稿人相比，子类别覆盖率提高了 81%。然而，合计子类别覆盖率平均仍仅为 40%，并且五个顶级类别的覆盖率仍然缺失，这表明潜在的不完整性仍然存在。After combining all reviewers, a paper covers 3.98 categories out of 5 and 9.23 subcategories out of 23 on average, which improves subcategory coverage by 81% compared to a single reviewer. However, the aggregated subcategory coverage is still only 40% on average, and missing coverage remains across five top-level categories, revealing that potential incompleteness still exists.

We further verify that the incompleteness can bias reference-based evaluation: AI opinions from categories absent in the human references receive systematically lower scores. Further conclusions are in Appendix B.1.

3.2.人类审稿人的不一致3.2. The Inconsistency of Human Reviewers

人工审核的可靠性也受到挑战。我们的分析揭示了不同审稿人之间以及审稿人和作者之间频繁发生的冲突，表明审稿可能包含错误的观点，并降低了其作为黄金参考文献的可靠性。案例如图3所示。The reliability of human review is also challenged. Our analysis reveals frequent conflicts between different reviewers and between reviewers and authors, indicating that reviews may contain erroneous opinions, and reducing their reliability as gold references. Cases are shown in Figure 3.

审稿人之间的冲突。Inter-Reviewer Conflicts.超过 13% 的提交材料显示最高和最低总分之间的分数差距为 4 或更多，这表明面试官冲突普遍存在。此外，为了检测错误的意见，我们汇总了讨论同一点的评论，并且当存在冲突时，我们使用元评论来指定正确和不正确的意见，详见§4。在 22.13% 的论文和 7.63% 的评论中，错误是通过审稿人之间的冲突发现的。这表明冲突Over 13% of submissions exhibit a score gap of 4 or more between the highest and lowest overall scores, demonstrating the prevalence of interreviewer conflicts. Furthermore, to detect erroneous opinions, we aggregate reviews that discuss the same point and, when conflicts exist, we use the meta-review to designate the correct and incorrect opinions, detailed in §4. In 22.13% of papers and 7.63% of reviews, incorrectness is identified via inter-reviewer conflicts. This demonstrates that conflicts

审稿人与作者之间的冲突。Reviewer-Author Conflicts.作者的回复也提供了宝贵的反馈。我们确定作者是否明确反对审稿人的意见，在这种情况下，我们使用元审稿来指定正确和不正确的意见。在 75.72% 的论文和 36.76% 的评论中，通过审稿人与作者之间的冲突发现了错误，这表明审稿人可能会犯错误。此外，为了提供更多见解，我们发现大多数分歧源于论文中的误解或遗漏细节，案例见附录 C.1。Author responses also provide valuable feedback. We determine whether authors explicitly oppose a reviewer’s opinion and, in such cases, we use the meta-review to designate the correct and incorrect opinions. In 75.72% of papers and 36.76% of reviews, incorrectness is identified via reviewer–author conflicts, demonstrating the potential errors made by reviewers. Furthermore, to provide more insight, we find that most disagreements stem from misunderstandings or missed details in the paper, with cases in Appendix C.1.

品类分布。Category Distribution.我们进一步发现不同类型的错误表现出显着不同的类别分布。审稿人之间差距最大的三个类别是原创性-方法、清晰度-写作和质量-实验。相比之下，审稿人与作者的冲突主要集中在论文质量上，详见附录 B.2。We further find that different types of errors exhibit significantly different category distributions. The three categories with the largest reviewer-toreviewer gaps are Originality-Method, Clarity-Writing, and Quality-Experiment. In contrast, reviewer–author conflicts mainly focus on paper Quality, detailed in Appendix B.2.

4.CoCoReviewBench搭建4. CoCoReviewBench Construction

为了解决人工审稿人的覆盖范围有限和偶尔出现错误导致其作为黄金参考不可靠的问题，我们构建了 CoCoReviewBench 来提高参考的完整性和正确性。我们在第 4.2 节中描述了数据构建工作流程，并在第 4.3 节中介绍了我们如何评估人工智能审阅者。To address the limited coverage and occasional errors of human reviewers, which make them unreliable as a gold reference, we construct CoCoReviewBench to improve the completeness and correctness of the reference. We describe the data construction workflow in §4.2, and introduce how we evaluate AI reviewers in §4.3.

4.1.设计原理4.1. Design Principle

我们的目标不是合成本质上比人类更好的评论。相反，我们对现有的人工评论进行注释和过滤，生成比任何单一评论更全面、更可靠的参考。如表1所示，先前的同行评审资源仅提供部分监督或评估信号，这激励我们的设计共同提高参考的完整性和正确性。Our goal is not to synthesize reviews that are intrinsically better than humans. Instead, we annotate and filter existing human reviews, producing references that are more comprehensive and reliable than any single review. As summarized in Table 1, prior peer-review resources provide only partial supervision or evaluation signals, motivating our design to jointly improve reference completeness and correctness.

类别级基准。Category-Level Benchmark.我们通过两种方式提高完整性。首先，我们汇总了多个审稿人的评论，将每篇论文的类别覆盖率从 5.10 增加到 9.23。此外，由于即使是汇总的评论也可能不完整，因此我们采用类别级别的指标。我们不是根据可能无法完全覆盖论文的总体参考进行评分，而是将 CoCoReviewBench 构建为特定类别子基准的集合，并且对于每个子基准，仅包含该类别中可用的人工评论的论文，这可以防止由于缺少参考而受到惩罚，并提供更细粒度的见解。We improve completeness in two ways. First, we aggregate reviews from multiple reviewers, increasing per-paper category coverage from 5.10 to 9.23. Besides, because even the aggregated reviews may be incomplete, we adopt a category-level metric. Rather than scoring against an overall reference that may not fully cover the paper, we construct CoCoReviewBench as a collection of category-specific sub-benchmarks and, for each, include only papers with human reviews available in that category, which prevents penalization due to missing references and provides more fine-grained insights.

基于冲突的错误验证。Conflict-Based Error Verification.为了提高正确性，一个幼稚的方法是请实力雄厚的法学硕士来判断错误。然而，法学硕士往往缺乏强大的领域专业知识，使得此类判断难以信任。然而，同行评审自然会提供专家注释信号：作者反驳、元评审以及评审者之间对同一问题的分歧。以上所有内容都可以被视为专家对人类评论正确性的注释。我们将意见冲突视为潜在错误的指标，并使用这些人类信号来识别不正确的评论。虽然这并不能保证删除所有不正确的评论，而且元评论只是一个粗略的判断信号而不是完美的预言，但它仍然比要求法学硕士判断正确性或使用原始的人工评论更可靠。To improve correctness, a naive approach is to ask strong LLMs to judge errors. However, LLMs often lack strong domain expertise, making such judgments hard to trust. Peer review, however, naturally provides expert annotation signals: author rebuttals, meta-reviews, and disagreements among reviewers about the same issue. All of the above can be viewed as expert annotations about the correctness of human reviews. We treat opinion conflicts as indicators of potential errors and use these human signals to identify incorrect reviews. While this does not guarantee removing all incorrect reviews, and the meta-review is only a coarse adjudication signal rather than a perfect oracle, it is still more reliable than asking LLMs to judge correctness or using raw human reviews.

通过代理框架进行预先注入。Prior Injection via an Agent Framework.由于任务很复杂，我们构建了一个代理框架来处理人工评论并手动指定每个代理的作用，将上述设计编码为提示和工作流程（Wang et al., 2025）。每个智能体专注于一个子任务来提高质量，如图4所示。此外，为了在类别级别评估AI评论，我们还需要对AI评论进行分类。为了降低成本，我们将代理工作流程提炼为 8B 模型以进行 AI 审阅分类。Because the task is complex, we build an agent framework to process human reviews and manually specify what each agent does, encoding the above designs into prompts and the workflow (Wang et al., 2025). Each agent focuses on one subtask to improve quality, as shown in Figure 4. In addition, to evaluate AI reviews at the category level, we also need to classify AI reviews. To reduce cost, we distill the agent workflow into 8B models for AI review classification.

潜在影响。Potential Impact.CoCoReviewBench 强调了人工评审的局限性，作为同行评审评估的参考CoCoReviewBench highlights limitations of human reviews as references for peer review evalu-

图 4.Figure 4.CoCoReviewBench 构建的工作流程。在§4.2.1中，我们将每条评论与相应作者的回复进行匹配，将其拆分为原子意见，并分配标签。这些结果在§4.2.2中进一步处理：步骤4和5检测审稿间冲突，步骤6检测审稿-作者冲突，产生最终的结构化数据。这些结构化数据可作为与人工智能评论进行比较的参考，这些评论也通过我们训练的模型进行分割和分类（§4.3）。Workflow of CoCoReviewBench construction. In §4.2.1, we match each review with the corresponding author response, split it into atomic opinions, and assign labels. These results are further processed in §4.2.2: Steps 4 and 5 detect inter-review conflicts, and Step 6 detects review-author conflicts, yielding the final structured data. This structured data serves as the reference for comparison with AI reviews, which are also split and classified by the model we trained (§4.3).

Besides, for training AI reviewers, suboptimal human supervision can also be harmful. Finally, thanks to the structured annotations of our benchmark, our analysis reveals shortcomings of existing AI reviewers and motivates further improvement.

4.2.基准数据建设4.2. Benchmark Data Construction

4.2.1.完整性感知分类4.2.1. COMPLETENESS-AWARE CLASSIFICATION

为了构建类别级基准，我们通过将审稿人反馈分解为原子意见并将这些意见分为 23 个子类别来注释所有人工审阅的标签，如附录 A 中所述。To build a category-level benchmark, we annotate labels for all human reviews by decomposing reviewer feedback into atomic opinions and classifying these opinions into 23 subcategories, as described in Appendix A.

查看细分和分类。Review Segmentation and Classification.原始评论通常是冗长的、非结构化的文本。一次将此类文本分类为多个类别组很容易引发幻觉。因此，我们将其分为两个步骤。我们首先在换行符和语义转换（例如“但是”、“进一步”）处应用基于规则的分段，同时限制每个分段的最小和最大标记长度。然后，我们要求法学硕士分配句子级标签，指示原子意见成员资格和相关作者响应，并将具有相同标签的句子聚合到原子讨论记录中。然后，对于每个原子意见，我们使用 LLM 请求对其进行分类，从而实现类别级别的评估。Raw reviews are often long, unstructured texts. Classifying such text into multiple category groups in a single pass can easily induce hallucinations. Therefore, we split it into two steps. We first apply rule-based segmentation at newlines and semantic transitions (e.g., “However,” “Furthermore”), while constraining the minimum and maximum token length of each segment. We then ask an LLM to assign sentencelevel labels indicating atomic-opinion membership and the associated author response, and aggregate sentences with the same label into atomic discussion records. Then, for each atomic opinion, we use an LLM request to classify it, enabling category-level evaluation.

二次细分。Secondary Segmentation.基于 LLM 的分割可能会出错，导致多个意见被错误地合并为一个意见。为了解决这个问题，对于分配给多个类别的原子意见，我们进行了二次验证。我们通过减少应用更细粒度的基于规则的分割LLM-based segmentation may err, causing multiple opinions to be incorrectly merged into a single opinion. To address this, for atomic opinions assigned to multiple categories, we perform a secondary verification. We apply finer-grained rule-based segmentation by reducing

允许的最大长度，用法学硕士重新分类每个部分，并沿着类别更改边界进行分割，以进一步确保意见原子性。the allowed maximum length, reclassifying each part with an LLM, and splitting along category-change boundaries to further ensure opinion atomicity.

4.2.2.正确性意识冲突解决4.2.2. CORRECTNESS-AWARE CONFLICT RESOLUTION

为了利用同行评审中隐含的专家注释信号，我们从两个来源验证正确性，包括其他审稿人对同一点的评估以及作者作为专家注释的回应。当检测到冲突时，我们使用元审查作为高层裁决信号来决定哪一方更可靠，并从参考文献中排除不正确的意见。此外，我们删除了多余的意见，以确保最终的参考文献仅包含正确的、非重复的意见。To leverage the expert annotation signals implicit in peer review, we verify correctness from two sources, including other reviewers’ assessments of the same point and the author response as expert annotation. When a conflict is detected, we use the meta-review as a high-level adjudication signal to decide which side is more reliable, and exclude the incorrect opinions from references. In addition, we remove redundant opinions to ensure that the final references contain only correct, non-duplicate opinions.

审稿人之间的冗余和冲突。Inter-Reviewer Redundancy and Conflict.我们汇总每个子类别中针对同一主题的讨论，并仅保留一个作为参考，以避免冗余和冲突。我们分三个步骤进行：（1）汇总有关同一主题的讨论； (2) 对于包含多个讨论的小组，确定意见是否存在冲突； (3) 如果存在冲突，则使用元审查来确定正确的意见。我们通过三个单独的法学硕士请求来实现上述内容。最后，当多个意见针对同一主题时，我们只保留最长的正确意见作为代表性参考，因为它通常包含最完整的理由和正确的结论。We aggregate discussions that target the same topic within each subcategory and retain only one as the reference to avoid redundancy and conflicts. We do so in three steps: (1) aggregate discussions about the same topic; (2) for groups containing more than one discussion, determine whether there is a conflict among opinions; (3) if a conflict exists, use the meta-review to determine the correct opinion. We implement the above with three separate LLM requests. Finally, when multiple opinions target the same topic, we only keep the longest correct opinion as the representative reference, as it typically contains the most complete justification and correct conclusion.

审稿人与作者的冲突。Reviewer-Author Conflict.审稿人和作者之间也可能出现冲突，即当作者明确指出审稿人的观点不正确时。我们遵循与上述相同的流程，处理审查意见及其相关内容Conflicts can also arise between reviewers and authors, i.e., when the author explicitly states that a reviewer’s opinion is incorrect. We follow the same process as above, treating a review opinion and its cor-

5

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

响应作者作为一个群体响应，首先（1）确定是否存在意见冲突，然后（2）使用元审查来确定正确的意见。被判断为不正确的意见不作为参考。responding author response as a group, first (1) determine whether there is an opinion conflict, and then (2) use the meta-review to decide the correct opinion. Opinions judged to be incorrect are not used as references.

4.2.3. COCOOREVIEWBENCH验证4.2.3. COCOREVIEWBENCH VERIFICATION

数据集统计。Dataset Statistics.我们从 NeurIPS (2021-2024) 和 ICLR (2017-2025) 中策划了一个大规模数据集，每年抽取 300 篇论文。每篇论文至少有三位独立审稿人，至少 75% 的审稿有作者回复，确保有足够的背景进行验证。我们在分数段之间使用均匀分布：我们将分数线性映射到 [0We curate a large-scale dataset from NeurIPS (2021-2024) and ICLR (2017-2025), sampling 300 papers per year. Each paper has at least three independent reviewers, and at least 75% of reviews have author responses, ensuring sufficient context for verification. We use a uniform distribution across score segments: we linearly map scores to [0,10] 并在每年内使用阈值 3/5/7 进行分层抽样，以平衡四个分数段的试卷。此外，我们还会验证 OpenReview 时间戳，以确保每个关联的 PDF 对应于早于评论发布的版本。如果没有早期的 PDF 可用，我们会在评论发布前九个月内从 arXiv 下载相应的纸质 PDF，以确保其与评论保持一致。10] and perform stratified sampling within each year using thresholds 3/5/7 to balance papers across four score segments. In addition, we verify OpenReview timestamps to ensure that each associated PDF corresponds to a version released earlier than the reviews. If no earlier PDF is available, we download the corresponding paper PDF from arXiv within nine months before the review release, ensuring its alignment with the reviews.

总共，我们得到In total, we obtain3,900论文，与papers, with14.1k审查意见，review comments,134.8k意见,opinions,115.9k意见集群，以及opinion clusters, and108.6k以正确的意见作为参考，构成第一个结构化数据集，包括原子意见分割、细粒度类别标签、相似评论聚类和错误意见注释，从而使人工智能审稿人的评估更加可靠。提示的详细信息参见附录 E.1。correct opinions as references, constituting the first structured dataset that includes atomic opinion segmentation, fine-grained category labels, similar-review clustering, and incorrect-opinion annotations, enabling more reliable evaluation of AI reviewers. Prompts are detailed in Appendix E.1.

逐步验证。Step-Level Verification.为了证明 CoCoReviewBench 的可靠性，在工作流程的每个步骤中，我们都使用了六种不同的强大的 LLM，并且遵循 Weng 等人的观点。 （2025），应用留一法交叉验证。我们发现六个模型之间的一致性相对较高，证明了工作流程的稳健性。同时，在每一步中，我们都会选择得分最高的模型，以最大化最终的基准质量。详细的度量定义、每个步骤的跨模型协议以及每个步骤的选择请参见附录 B.3。To demonstrate the reliability of CoCoReviewBench, at each step in the workflow, we use six different strong LLMs and, following Weng et al. (2025), apply leave-one-out cross-validation. We find relatively high agreement among the six models, demonstrating the robustness of the workflow. Meanwhile, at each step, we select the highest-scoring model to maximize the final benchmark quality. Detailed metric definitions, cross-model agreement at each step, and per-step choices are in Appendix B.3.

人工验证。Human Verification.我们从最终基准中随机抽取 50 篇论文，并聘请机器学习博士生进行注释。然后，我们计算每个注释步骤的准确性，该准确性按相应评论中的单词数进行加权。对于评论分类，85.45% 的类别完全正确。对于意见汇总，93.41% 是正确的。对于审稿人之间冲突引起的错误检测，我们达到了 81.40% 的准确率。这些高精度证明了我们基准测试的可靠性。对于由审稿人与作者冲突引起的错误检测，我们达到了 66.83% 的准确率。这种准确性较低，因为当元评论总体上是负面的时，注释者倾向于支持评论者而不是作者，特别是当元评论没有为特定观点提供详细的理由时。对于被拒绝的论文，注释准确率仅为 50.03%，而对于接受的论文We randomly sample 50 papers from the final benchmark and hire PhD students in machine learning to annotate. We then compute accuracy for each annotation step, which is weighted by the number of words in its corresponding review. For review classification, 85.45% have entirely correct categories. For opinion aggregation, 93.41% are correct. For error detection caused by inter-reviewer conflicts, we achieve 81.40% accuracy. These high accuracies demonstrate the reliability of our benchmark. For error detection caused by reviewer-author conflicts, we achieve 66.83% accuracy. This accuracy is lower, because when the meta-review is overall negative, annotators tend to favor the reviewer rather than the author, especially when the meta-review does not provide a detailed justification for the specific point. For rejected papers, the annotation accuracy is only 50.03%, while for accepted pa-

个人认为是 79.76%。我们在附录 C.2 中提供了案例研究来支持我们注释的可靠性。pers it is 79.76%. We provide case studies in Appendix C.2 to support the reliability of our annotations.

4.3. AI审稿人评价4.3. AI Reviewer Evaluation

为了使用我们的分类基准评估人工智能评论，我们还需要对它们进行分类。然而，调用强大的法学硕士来对每个人工智能评论进行分类的成本很高。因此，我们训练一个较小的 LLM，即 Qwen3-8B（Yang 等人，2025），对第 4.2.1 节中的人类审查执行相同的程序，并使用上一节的结果数据作为训练监督。由于这个阶段不需要处理作者回复，所以比较容易，并且8B模型可以表现良好。To evaluate AI reviews with our categorical benchmark, we also need to classify them. However, invoking strong LLMs to classify every AI review is expensive. Therefore, we train a smaller LLM, i.e., Qwen3-8B (Yang et al., 2025), to perform the same procedure on human reviews in §4.2.1, and use the resulting data of the previous section as training supervision. Since this stage does not need to handle author replies, it is easier, and an 8B model can perform well.

回顾细分。Review Segmentation.在这里，我们输入AI评论句子，让模型聚合表达相同原子观点的句子。对于训练，数据集很小，因为每篇论文仅提供一个数据实例。然而，奖励是密集的：任意两个句子是否属于同一个原子意见都可以被视为一个决定。因此，我们采用 GRPO (Shao et al., 2024)，每个实例生成 32 条轨迹来增强数据，并使用 Omega Index (Lm & Cw, 1988) 作为奖励，用于评估聚类的正确性。最终的奖励是：Here, we input AI review sentences and let the model aggregate sentences that express the same atomic opinion. For training, the dataset is small since each paper contributes only one data instance. However, the reward is dense: whether any two sentences belong to the same atomic opinion can be seen as one decision. We therefore adopt GRPO (Shao et al., 2024), generating 32 trajectories per instance to augment the data, and use the Omega Index (Lm & Cw, 1988) as the reward, which is used to evaluate clustering correctness. The final reward is:

R= 最大值(0= max(0. 5 ,OmegaIndex + 1（正确格式））OmegaIndex + 1 (Correct Format)). (1)

我们在基准构建中使用二次分割的结果进行训练，得到模型We train using results of secondary segmentation in benchmark construction, and obtain the model评论分割ReviewSplit用于人工智能评论细分。for AI review segmentation.

审查分类。Review Classification.然后，我们进一步对这些原子意见进行分类。对于训练来说，数据集很大，因为每个意见对应一个数据实例。然而，奖励稀疏，只有正确/错误。如果我们使用 GRPO，大多数组内的结果都是相同的，从而阻止有效更新。因此，我们使用指令调优：训练Qwen3-8B的非思维模式，观察到它的思维模式能力也有所提高。我们最终得到模型Then, we further classify these atomic opinions. For training, the dataset is large since each opinion corresponds to one data instance. However, the reward is sparse, only correct / incorrect. If we use GRPO, results are the same within most groups, preventing effective updates. Thus, we use instruction tuning: we train the non-thinking mode of Qwen3-8B, and observe that its thinking-mode capability also improves. We finally obtain the model评论分类ReviewClassify用于AI评论分类。for AI review classification.

可靠性。Reliability.为了验证上述模型的可靠性，我们首先通过在训练前后比较六位强LLM与Qwen3-8B进行阶梯评估，发现我们训练的模型明显强于Qwen3-8B，接近甚至超越一些强LLM的表现，详见附录B.3。我们进一步进行人工注释，发现该模型在 87.09% 的评论中产生完全正确的分类，证明了我们模型的有效性。To validate the reliability of the above models, we first perform step-level evaluation by comparing the six strong LLMs with Qwen3-8B before and after training, and find that our trained model is substantially stronger than Qwen3-8B and approaches, or even surpasses, the performance of some strong LLMs, detailed in Appendix B.3. We further conduct human annotation and find that the model produces entirely correct classifications in 87.09% of the reviews, demonstrating the effectiveness of our model.

5. 实验5. Experiments

通过细粒度的数据清理、步骤级质量验证和人工注释，我们构建了可靠的人工参考。在此基础上，我们利用值得信赖的Through fine-grained data cleaning, step-level quality validation, and human annotation, we construct reliable human references. Building on this, we leverage our trustworthy

6

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

参考文献和细粒度分类的优势，对现有人工智能审稿人的优势和局限性进行更详细的分析，激励未来人工智能审稿人的发展。references and the advantages of fine-grained categorization to conduct a more detailed analysis of the strengths and limitations of existing AI reviewers, motivating future AI reviewer development.

5.1.实验装置5.1. Experimental Setup

指标。Metrics.我们关注 Tan 等人。 （2024）和张等人。 (2025)，使用旧的词汇/语义相似性指标，包括 BLEU、ROUGE-L 和 BERTScore 与 RoBERTalarge (Liu et al., 2019)，将人工智能评论与每个评论者的回答进行比较，并取最大值作为最终分数。然而，由于这些评估 AI 审稿人的指标存在局限性，受到 Garg 等人的启发。 (2025) 和 Sadallah 等人。 （2025），我们设计了一个专门针对类别级别评估的法学硕士法官协议，以实现更全面、更可靠的评估。具体来说，我们首先使用上面训练的8B模型将AI评论分解为原子意见并分配子类别标签。然后，我们将每个类别下的人工智能评论与人类参考进行比较，获得 1 到 5 的分数。评估维度包括：We follow Tan et al. (2024) and Zhang et al. (2025), using old lexical/semantic similarity metrics, including BLEU, ROUGE-L, and BERTScore with RoBERTalarge (Liu et al., 2019), comparing the AI review with each reviewer’s response and taking the maximum as the final score. However, due to the limitations of these metrics for evaluating AI reviewers, inspired by Garg et al. (2025) and Sadallah et al. (2025), we design an LLM-as-a-judge protocol tailored to category-level evaluation for a more comprehensive and reliable assessment. Specifically, we first use the 8B model we trained above to decompose AI reviews into atomic opinions and assign subcategory labels. We then compare AI reviews with human references under each category, obtaining a score from 1 to 5. Evaluation dimensions include:

  • 正确性Correctness：衡量候选人评论评论与我们基准中的人类观点（作为黄金参考）的一致性。通过删除冲突和高度相似的评论，我们为每个集群保留一个最可靠的原子意见作为参考。: Measures how well a candidate review comment aligns with the human opinions in our benchmark as gold references. By removing conflicting and highly similar comments, we retain a single most reliable atomic opinion per cluster as the reference.

  • 彻底性Thoroughness：衡量评论讨论当前类别的全面程度，通过候选者覆盖人工评论的完全程度进行量化。: Measures how comprehensively the comment discusses the current category, quantified by how completely the candidate covers the human reviews.

  • 接地Grounding：衡量审稿意见提及论文特定部分的明确程度以及该部分问题的明确程度。: Measures how explicitly a review comment refers to a specific part of the paper and how clearly it identifies the issue with that part.

  • 可验证性Verifiability：评估是否可以根据提供的推理、常识或外部参考来验证某个主张，确保评论有助于作者改进他们的工作。: Assesses whether a claim can be verified based on the provided reasoning, common knowledge, or external references, ensuring that the comment helps authors improve their work.

  • 清晰度Clarity：判断评论的连贯性、可读性和结构是否良好。: Judges how coherent, readable, and wellstructured the comment is.

我们仅在人类和人工智能都提供该类别的评论时进行评估，评估现有人工智能评论的正确性。虽然上述指标侧重于We evaluate only when both the human and AI provide comments in that category, assessing the correctness of existing AI comments. While the above metrics focus on品类内质量intracategory quality，我们另外评估, we additionally evaluate类别完整性category completeness测量to measure类别间覆盖inter-category coverage。具体来说，我们将所有审稿人涵盖的类别的并集视为满分（100），并计算人工智能审稿每篇论文涵盖的平均类别数与该论文所有审稿人涵盖的类别数的比率作为最终得分。只有两者都获得高分才表明评价更好。此外，. Specifically, we treat the union of categories covered by all reviewers as the full score (100), and compute the ratio of the average number of categories covered per paper by the AI review to that covered by all reviewers of that paper as the final score. Only high scores on both indicate a better review. Further,纸质指标paper-level metric使用相同的提示，但通过提供所有类别的人工智能评论和人类评论一次对论文进行评分uses the same prompt but scores a paper once by feeding all categories’ AI reviews and human

一起参考，而类别级评估则对每个类别独立评分并对结果进行平均。references together, while category-level evaluation scores each category independently and averages the results.

此外，为了提供可靠的参考分数，我们使用人类表现。具体来说，我们进行Besides, to provide reliable reference scores, we use human performance. Concretely, we conduct留一验证leave-one-out validation通过随机选择一个人类评论作为“候选者”，使用剩余的评论作为参考。所有对人工智能评论的评估都同样使用剩余的集合作为参考。我们使用温度为0的GPT-5-Mini作为判断，提示如附录E.2所示。对于所有实验，我们从包含 3,900 篇论文的完整数据集中随机抽取三分之一，每年 100 篇论文，同时保留原始分数分布。该抽样对分数的影响可以忽略不计，如附录 B.4 所示。by randomly selecting one human review as the “candidate”, using the remaining reviews as references. All evaluations of AI reviews similarly use the remaining set as references. We use GPT-5-Mini with temperature 0 as the judge, with prompts shown in Appendix E.2. For all experiments, we randomly sample one third of the full dataset of 3,900 papers, with 100 papers per year, while preserving the original score distribution. This sampling has a negligible impact on scores, as shown in Appendix B.4.

评估模型。Evaluated Models.我们评估四组模型。首先，我们包括强大的闭源模型：GPT-5.2、GPT-5-Mini、Gemini-3-Pro 和 Gemini-3-Flash。其次，我们评估通用开源推理模型，包括 QwQ-32B（Team，2025）、Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-32B（Yang 等人，2025）以及 Nemotron-3-30BA3B（NVIDIA，2025）。第三，我们评估了通用的开源非推理模型，包括 Llama-3.3-70B、Llama-3.1-8B (Dubey et al., 2024)、Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen-2.5-7B (Qwen et al., 2024) 的非思维模式。最后，我们评估了专门的人工智能审稿模型，包括 DeepReviewer-7B 和 DeepReviewer-14B (Zhu et al., 2025b)、CycleReviewer-8B 和 CycleReviewer-70B (Weng et al., 2025)、OpenReviewer-8B (Idahl & Ahmadi, 2025) 和 SEA-E-7B (Yu et al., 2024)。在专门的AI审稿模型中，只有DeepReviewer是推理模型；其他是非推理模型。We evaluate four groups of models. First, we include strong closed-source models: GPT-5.2, GPT-5-Mini, Gemini-3-Pro, and Gemini-3-Flash. Second, we evaluate general open-source reasoning models, including QwQ-32B (Team, 2025), Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B (Yang et al., 2025), and Nemotron-3-30BA3B (NVIDIA, 2025). Third, we evaluate general opensource non-reasoning models, including Llama-3.3-70B, Llama-3.1-8B (Dubey et al., 2024), the non-thinking mode of Qwen3-8B, and Qwen-2.5-7B (Qwen et al., 2024). Finally, we evaluate specialized AI reviewer models, including DeepReviewer-7B and DeepReviewer-14B (Zhu et al., 2025b), CycleReviewer-8B and CycleReviewer-70B (Weng et al., 2025), OpenReviewer-8B (Idahl & Ahmadi, 2025), and SEA-E-7B (Yu et al., 2024). Among the specialized AI reviewer models, only DeepReviewer is a reasoning model; the others are non-reasoning models.

对于所有通用模型，我们使用 Lu 等人的提示。 (2024)，设置最大生成长度为32k，使用温度0.6。对于专门的人工智能审稿人模型，我们使用原始论文中的默认生成设置。如果生成的评论不遵循所需的模板，我们将重新生成一次。如果第二代仍然无法遵循模板，我们将保留完整的输出。其他设置详见附录 D。For all general-purpose models, we use the prompt from Lu et al. (2024), set the maximum generation length to 32k, and use temperature 0.6. For specialized AI reviewer models, we use the default generation settings from their original papers. If a generated review does not follow the required template, we regenerate it once. If the second generation still fails to follow the template, we keep the full output. Other settings are detailed in Appendix D.

5.2.主要实验5.2. Main Experiments

旧指标的事实正确性的语义重叠。Semantic Overlap over Factual Correctness of Old Metrics.完整结果如表 2 所示。根据旧指标，一些得分最高的系统是非推理模型或经过人类评论训练的专门人工智能审稿模型。然而，它们的性能甚至优于 GPT-5 和 Gemini-3 系列，后者的规模更大、功能更强大。这表明旧指标主要奖励语义级别的匹配，而不是实际的正确性和有用性。相比之下，我们以LLM为评委的论文级指标（Paper.）提供了更合理的分数，表明这是一个更适合AI审稿人的评估方案。Full results are in Table 2. Under the old metrics, several of the highest-scoring systems are non-reasoning models or specialized AI reviewer models trained on human reviews. However, they even outperform the GPT-5 and Gemini-3 families, which are substantially larger and more capable. This indicates that the old metrics primarily reward semantic-level matching rather than actual correctness and usefulness. In contrast, our paper-level metrics with LLM-as-a-judge (Paper.) provide more reasonable scores, demonstrating that this is a more suitable evaluation scheme for AI reviewers.

更清晰但不太正确和彻底的人工智能评论。Clearer but Less Correct and Thorough AI Reviews.此外，我们的品类级多维度评估可以实现更细粒度的分析。为了与人类参考资料保持一致（正确）以及人工智能评论对这一类别的讨论程度（Thoro），这两个维度总体上仍然相对较弱：更强的闭源模型可以超过人类评论者，但许多其他模型的得分仍然低于人类评论者。相比之下，它们的清晰度得分始终高于人类评论的得分。这表明当前的人工智能评审系统在核心评估标准上仍然表现不佳。Further, our category-level multi-dimensional evaluation enables a finer-grained analysis. For consistency with human references (Correct.) and how thorough AI reviews discuss this category (Thoro.), these two dimensions remain relatively weaker overall: the stronger closed-source models can exceed human reviewers, but many other models still score lower than human reviewers. In contrast, their clarity scores are consistently higher than those of human reviews. This suggests that current AI reviewer systems still underperform on core evaluation criteria.

推理模型中的人性化基础和可验证性。Human-Level Grounding and Verifiability in Reasoning Models.基础性和可验证性衡量模型是否能够清楚地提供其审查的来源和理由。结果表明，推理模型具有显着的优势，可以与人类评审员相媲美，甚至更好。这表明现代 LRM 可以生成有充分支持的评论，通常比人类更详细。相比之下，非推理模型的表现要差得多，这凸显了推理的必要性。Grounding and verifiability measure whether a model can clearly provide the source and rationale for its reviews. The results show that reasoning models have a significant advantage and are comparable to, or even better than, human reviewers. This indicates that modern LRMs can generate well-supported reviews, often in more detail than humans. In contrast, non-reasoning models perform substantially worse, highlighting the necessity of reasoning.

人工智能评论中的类别覆盖范围更广但更浅。Broader but Shallower Category Coverage in AI Reviews.跨类别，我们发现人工智能评论通常比单个人类评论覆盖更多类别（完整），特别是推理模型，但比单一人类评论覆盖的类别要少。Across categories, we find that AI reviews often cover more categories than a single human review (Complete.), especially reasoning models, but fewer than the

所有评论者的得分始终低于 100 分。此外，在每个类别中，人工智能评论平均而言并没有在每个类别中提供更好的彻底分析，尤其是在非思维模型上。这说明，与人类相比，人工智能评论的品类覆盖范围更广，但深度不足，广度仍有提升空间。此外，我们构建了一个简单的代理框架，可以确保完整的类别覆盖而不降低正确性，并有可能综合更全面的参考，如附录 B.5 中详述。aggregation of all reviewers, with their scores consistently below 100. Further, within each category, AI reviews provide no better thorough analysis within each category on average, especially on non-thinking models. This shows that, compared to humans, AI reviews have broader category coverage but insufficient depth, and their breadth still has room for improvement. Further, we build a simple agent framework that can ensure full category coverage without reducing correctness, with potential to synthesize more comprehensive references, as detailed in Appendix B.5.

元评审裁决分析Meta-Review Adjudication Analysis我们基于冲突的过滤依赖于元评论作为高级裁决信号。因此，我们进一步分析元评论是否在冲突场景中提供可靠的指导。我们考虑具有明确冲突信号的论文，并使用与§5.1中相同的LLM作为法官设置在四个1-5维度上评估原始元评审。Our conflict-based filtering relies on meta-reviews as high-level adjudication signals. We therefore further analyze whether meta-reviews provide reliable guidance in conflict scenarios. We consider papers with explicit conflict signals and evaluate the original meta-review on four 1–5 dimensions using the same LLM-as-a-judge setting as in §5.1.裁决清晰Adjudication Clarity衡量元审查是否对主要争议问题表明了明确的立场。measures whether the meta-review states a clear stance on the main disputed issue.接地气Groundedness衡量这一立场是否得到审稿人评论和讨论背景的支持。measures whether this stance is supported by reviewer comments and discussion context.可验证性Verifiability衡量判断是否足够具体以供检验。measures whether the judgment is concrete enough to be checked.冲突报道Conflict Coverage衡量元审查是否解决了主要争议点，而不是仅解决了其中的一部分。measures whether the meta-review addresses the major disputed points rather than only part of them.

8

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

图 5.Figure 5.结果按类别细分如表 2 所示。Category-wise breakdown of the results in Table 2.顶部：Top:模型的观点涵盖每个类别的百分比。the percentage of a model’s opinions that cover each category.底部：Bottom:类别正确性（左）和彻底性分数（右）（1-5）。所有类别均按 AI 得分减去人类得分从左到右排序，因此左侧的类别表示 AI 性能更强。the category-wise correctness (left) and thoroughness scores (right) (1–5). All categories are ordered from left to right by the score of AI minus that of humans, so categories on the left indicate stronger AI performance.

我们将强冲突论文定义为至少检测到五个不正确观点的论文。如表3所示，强冲突论文在所有四个维度上的得分都高于弱冲突论文，平均得分从2.94增加到3.24。此外，73.1% 的强冲突论文的四维平均得分至少为 3，这表明当冲突严重时，元评论通常会提供有关主要争议的有意义的高级信号。然而，得分仍远未达到完美，尤其是在冲突报道方面。这证实了元评论应该被视为粗略的裁决信号，而不是每个细节争议的细粒度地面事实。We define strong-conflict papers as papers with at least five detected incorrect opinions. As shown in Table 3, strongconflict papers receive higher scores than weak-conflict papers across all four dimensions, with the average score increasing from 2.94 to 3.24. Moreover, 73.1% of strongconflict papers have a four-dimensional average score of at least 3, suggesting that meta-reviews usually provide a meaningful high-level signal about the main dispute when conflicts are substantial. However, the scores remain far from perfect, especially on Conflict Coverage. This confirms that meta-reviews should be treated as coarse adjudication signals rather than fine-grained ground truth for every detailed dispute.

5.3. AI审稿人的分类分析5.3. Categorical Analysis of AI Reviewers

为了诊断表 2 中的差距来自何处，我们按类别对结果进行了细分，以提供进一步的见解。To diagnose where the gaps in Table 2 come from, we break down the results by category to provide further insights.

更广泛的主要类别覆盖范围。Broader Major-Category Coverage.图 5 的上图报告了类别完整性：我们计算每篇论文至少一次涵盖每个类别的模型意见的百分比。人工智能审稿在质量相关类别以及人类很少涉及的一些类别上优于人类，这表明人工智能审稿人更关注质量相关类别。令人惊讶的是，虽然我们不提供图像作为输入，但覆盖范围The upper panel in Figure 5 reports category completeness: we compute the percentage of a model’s opinions that cover each category at least once for each paper. AI reviews outperform humans on Quality-related categories, as well as on some categories that humans rarely cover, indicating that AI reviewers pay more attention to Quality-related categories. Surprisingly, although we do not provide images as input, the coverage for

与人物相关的类别也很高，表明存在潜在的幻觉风险，我们稍后进行分析。Figure-related categories is also high, suggesting potential hallucination risks, which we analyze later.

质量绩效强劲，但政策和清晰度薄弱。Strong Quality Performance but Weak Policy and Clarity.图 5 的底部显示了正确性和彻底性的分数。两者的高分表明表现强劲。在主要类别层面，质量是人工智能审稿人最强的维度，并且已经接近或略高于人类基线。原创性和重要性更加复杂，但仍然相对接近人类的平均表现。相比之下，最明显的弱点出现在清晰度方面，尤其是政策方面，人工智能审稿人在正确性和彻底性方面都落后于人类。这表明剩余的主要差距不再均匀分布在所有类别中，而是集中在几个主要维度。其他维度得分的进一步分析见附录B.6。The bottom of Figure 5 shows the scores for correctness and thoroughness. High scores on both indicate strong performance. At the major-category level, Quality is the strongest dimension for AI reviewers and is already close to, or slightly above, the human baseline. Originality and Significance are more mixed but remain relatively close to human performance on average. In contrast, the clearest weaknesses appear in Clarity and especially Policy, where AI reviewers lag behind humans in both correctness and thoroughness. This suggests that the main remaining gaps are no longer uniformly spread across all categories, but are concentrated in a few major dimensions. Further analysis of scores in other dimensions is in Appendix B.6.

人工智能审稿人的幻觉风险。Hallucination Risks for AI Reviewers.在所有实验中，我们都使用纯文本输入。然而，我们发现语言模型偶尔会产生与图形相关的意见。尽管概率很低（每篇论文少于 0.05 个意见），但这种情况在每个模型中都会发生，表明存在幻觉。此外，我们将被判断为不正确的观点作为参考，发现人类和人工智能在正确性上的差距大幅缩小，这表明人工智能也可能适合这种错误的观点，导致潜在的幻觉，案例和分析见附录B.7。In all the experiments, we use text-only inputs. However, we find that language models occasionally generate figure-related opinions. Although the probability is low—fewer than 0.05 opinions per paper—this occurs in every model, indicating hallucinations. Further, we treat the opinions judged as incorrect as references and find that the gap between humans and AI in correctness narrows substantially, demonstrating that AI may also fit such erroneous opinions, leading to potential hallucinations, with cases and analysis in Appendix B.7.

六、结论6. Conclusion

我们推出了 CoCoReviewBench，这是一个以完整性和正确性为导向的基准，用于评估 AI 审阅者。它支持类别级别的评估，避免因缺少人类参考而受到不公平的处罚，并通过利用审稿人-作者-元讨论来过滤错误意见来提高正确性。实验表明，传统指标可能会产生误导，而且对于人工智能审阅者来说，正确性仍然是相对较弱的维度，尤其是在最强大的闭源模型之外。我们还提倡使用推理模型作为人工智能评审器，它在大多数指标上表现更好。我们希望我们可靠的基准和进一步的见解能够促进人工智能审稿人的发展。We introduce CoCoReviewBench, a completeness- and correctness-oriented benchmark for evaluating AI reviewers. It enables category-level evaluation that avoids unfair penalties from missing human references, and improves correctness by leveraging reviewer–author–meta discussions to filter erroneous opinions. Experiments show that traditional metrics can be misleading, and that correctness remain relatively weaker dimensions for AI reviewers, especially outside the strongest closed-source models. We also advocate using reasoning models as AI reviewers, which perform better on most metrics. We hope our reliable benchmark and further insights facilitate AI reviewer development.

局限性。Limitations.首先，基于冲突的过滤仅检测表面上表现为明显分歧的错误，并且还可能带有情绪框架，导致在缺乏回应或过于温和时错过错误意见。然而，这可能仍然比基于法学硕士的判断更可靠，因为后者往往缺乏及时的领域专业知识。此外，我们的评估需要额外的计算来对人工智能评论进行分段和分类。我们通过将流程提炼为 8B 模型来缓解这一问题。最后，除了提供更完整的反馈之外，法学硕士还应该能够识别最关键的评论，而这在很大程度上仍未被探索。First, conflict-based filtering only detects errors that surface as explicit disagreements, and can also carry sentiment framing, causing erroneous opinions to be missed when responses are absent or overly mild. However, this may still be more reliable than LLM-based judgments, which often lack timely domain expertise. Besides, our evaluation requires additional computation to segment and categorize AI reviews. We mitigate this by distilling the pipeline into 8B models. Finally, beyond providing more complete feedback, LLMs should also be able to identify the most critical reviews, which remains largely unexplored.

9

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

影响报告Impact Statement

我们的工作促进了人工智能评审系统在多个维度的开发和可靠评估。Our work facilitates the development and reliable evaluation of AI reviewer systems in multiple dimensions.

  • 我们强调人工评论的局限性，包括类别覆盖不完整和存在冲突，这表明错误的意见并使人工评论作为训练数据和评估参考都不是最佳的。这个问题在之前的人工智能审稿器开发中经常被忽视。我们呼吁更多地关注这一限制，这可能最终推动人工智能审稿人超越人类的表现。We highlight limitations of human reviews, including incomplete category coverage and the presence of conflicts, which indicate erroneous opinions and make human reviews suboptimal as both training data and evaluation references. This issue has often been overlooked in prior AI reviewer development. We call for greater attention to this limitation, which may ultimately propel AI reviewers to surpass human performance.

  • 我们提出了CoCoReviewBench，它通过更完整、更细粒度的评估协议来解决这些问题，提高AI审稿人评估的可靠性。我们希望它能为未来人工智能评审器的发展提供更可靠、标准化的基准，并能够在不同的人工智能评审器系统之间进行公平的比较。We propose CoCoReviewBench, which addresses these issues with a more complete and fine-grained evaluation protocol, improving the reliability of AI reviewer evaluation. We hope it can provide a more reliable and standardized benchmark for future AI reviewer development and enable fair comparisons across different AI reviewer systems.

  • 我们的细粒度分析表明，当前的人工智能审稿人在正确性和彻底性方面仍然落后于人类，特别是对于非质量类别，并且可能会表现出幻觉。同时，推理模型具有更强的基础性和可验证性，为未来人工智能审稿人的发展提供了动力。Our fine-grained analysis shows that current AI reviewers still lag behind humans in correctness and thoroughness, especially for non-Quality categories, and can exhibit hallucinations. Meanwhile, reasoning models achieve stronger grounding and verifiability, motivating the use of reasoning models for future AI reviewer development.

最后，在道德方面，法学硕士可能会表现出种族和性别偏见，因此我们强烈建议用户在特定情况下应用模型之前评估潜在的偏见。此外，由于LLM输出难以控制，用户应警惕幻觉引起的问题。Finally, for ethical aspects, LLMs may exhibit racial and gender biases, so we strongly recommend users assess potential biases before applying the models in specific contexts. Additionally, due to the difficulty of controlling LLM outputs, users should be cautious of issues arising from hallucinations.

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

A. 同行评审分类法A. Peer Review Taxonomy

为了全面评估同行评审的内容并构建分类子基准，我们开发了一个由 5 个顶级类别和 23 个子类别组成的分层分类法。这种更细的粒度可以更精确地诊断人工智能评论，并促进更详细的评论分析，从而产生更多见解。To comprehensively evaluate the content of peer reviews and construct categorical sub-benchmarks, we develop a hierarchical taxonomy consisting of 5 top-level categories with 23 subcategories. This finer granularity enables more precise diagnosis of AI reviews, and facilitates more detailed review analysis that yields additional insights.

A.1.层次分类定义A.1. Hierarchical Taxonomy Definitions

为了构建一套可靠且全面的类别，我们在很大程度上遵循 NeurIPS 2025 审稿人指南[1] 和 ACL 滚动审稿审稿表[2]。我们定义了五个顶级类别，前四个类别来自 NeurIPS 2025，而最后一个类别在 ACL 滚动评审审核表中提到，但不包含在前四个类别中。To build a reliable and comprehensive set of categories, we largely follow the NeurIPS 2025 Reviewer Guidelines[1] and the ACL Rolling Review Review Form[2] . We define five top-level categories as follows: the first four are from NeurIPS 2025, while the last category is mentioned in the ACL Rolling Review Review Form but not covered by the first four categories.

品质Quality: 提交的技术是否合理？主张是否得到充分支持（例如，通过理论分析或实验结果）？使用的方法是否合适？这是一项完整的工作还是正在进行中的工作？作者是否认真、诚实地评估其作品的优点和缺点？: Is the submission technically sound? Are claims well supported (e.g., by theoretical analysis or experimental results)? Are the methods used appropriate? Is this a complete piece of work or work in progress? Are the authors careful and honest about evaluating both the strengths and weaknesses of their work?

清晰度Clarity: 提交的内容写清楚了吗？组织得好吗？ （如果没有，请提出建设性建议以提高其清晰度。）它是否充分告知读者？ （请注意，一篇写得很好的论文为专家读者提供了足够的信息来重现其结果。）: Is the submission clearly written? Is it well organized? (If not, please make constructive suggestions for improving its clarity.) Does it adequately inform the reader? (Note that a superbly written paper provides enough information for an expert reader to reproduce its results.)

意义Significance：结果对社区有影响吗？其他人（研究人员或实践者）是否可能使用这些想法或在此基础上进行构建？提交的内容是否比以前的工作更好地解决了一项艰巨的任务？它是否以明显的方式增进了我们对该主题的理解/知识？它是否提供了独特的数据、关于现有数据的独特结论，或者独特的理论或实验方法？: Are the results impactful for the community? Are others (researchers or practitioners) likely to use the ideas or build on them? Does the submission address a difficult task in a better way than previous work? Does it advance our understanding/knowledge on the topic in a demonstrable way? Does it provide unique data, unique conclusions about existing data, or a unique theoretical or experimental approach?

原创性Originality：这项工作是否提供了新的见解、加深了理解或强调了现有方法的重要属性？这项工作与之前的贡献有何不同，并提供了相关引文，是否清楚？这项工作是否引入了推动该领域发展的新颖任务或方法？这项工作是否提供了现有技术的新颖组合，并且这种组合背后的推理是否清楚地阐明了？正如上述问题所表明的，原创性并不一定需要引入全新的方法。相反，通过评估现有方法提供新颖见解或证明效率、公平性提高等的工作也同样有价值。: Does the work provide new insights, deepen understanding, or highlight important properties of existing methods? Is it clear how this work differs from previous contributions, with relevant citations provided? Does the work introduce novel tasks or methods that advance the field? Does this work offer a novel combination of existing techniques, and is the reasoning behind this combination well articulated? As the questions above indicate, originality does not necessarily require introducing an entirely new method. Rather, work that provides novel insights by evaluating existing methods, or demonstrates improved efficiency, fairness, etc., is also equally valuable.

政策Policy：包含与政策或合规性相关的问题，例如道德、数据/隐私合规性、匿名规则、剽窃、许可和更广泛的影响。这些问题通常需要检查会议或法律政策的遵守情况，并且可能涉及道德考虑。: Encompasses policy- or compliance-related concerns such as ethics, data/privacy compliance, anonymity rules, plagiarism, licensing, and broader impact. These issues often require checking adherence to conference or legal policies and may involve ethical considerations.

为了实现细粒度的评估、训练和分析，我们进一步将这 5 个维度分解为 23 个细粒度的子类别，形成我们的分层分类法。这种细粒度的分解支持对审稿覆盖率进行更严格和系统的评估，确保科学稿件的每个关键方面都得到明确评估。详细定义如表 4 所示。To enable fine-grained evaluation, training, and analysis, we further decompose these 5 dimensions into 23 fine-grained subcategories, forming our hierarchical taxonomy. This granular decomposition supports a more rigorous and systematic assessment of review coverage, ensuring that each critical aspect of a scientific manuscript is explicitly evaluated. The detailed definitions are presented in Table 4.

A.2.详细施工流程A.2. Detailed Construction Process

为了构建子类别，我们主要依靠代理模式下的 GPT-5 数据挖掘，从大规模的现有数据中提取尽可能全面的子类别。具体来说，以上述审稿人指南为范例，我们首先提示模型从 NeurIPS、ICLR 和 ACL Rolling Review 等场所检索审稿人指南，以形成初始分类草案。我们通过此过程生成多个候选版本。然后，我们通过要求 GPT-5 和 Gemini-2.5-Pro 在候选分类法下对评论评论进行分类，计算分类一致性（模型间一致性），并选择一致性最高的版本，在小型开发集上评估每个版本。To construct the subcategories, we primarily rely on data mining via GPT-5 in agent mode to extract subcategories that are as comprehensive as possible from large-scale existing data. Concretely, using the reviewer guidelines above as exemplars, we first prompt the model to retrieve reviewer guidelines from venues such as NeurIPS, ICLR, and ACL Rolling Review to form an initial taxonomy draft. We generate multiple candidate versions with this procedure. We then evaluate each version on a small development set by asking GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-Pro to classify review comments under the candidate taxonomy, compute classification consistency (inter-model agreement), and select the version with the highest consistency.

接下来，我们通过让 GPT-5 代理模式收集大量 OpenReview 评论并尝试将它们分类到当前分类法下，进一步细化上述分类法。此过程有助于识别缺失和经常混淆的类别，促进分类法的进一步细化并添加边界规则以消除边界情况的歧义。我们还将 GPT-5 和 Gemini-2.5-Pro 在测试集上不一致的情况作为附加信号反馈到细化循环中。最后，我们最终确定了 23 个不同的子类别。类别集固定后，我们继续细化和添加边界规则，以减少模型混乱并产生最终的分类提示。Next, we further refine the above taxonomy by having GPT-5 agent mode collect large volumes of OpenReview reviews and attempt to categorize them under the current taxonomy. This process helps identify missing and frequently confused categories, prompting further taxonomy refinement and the addition of boundary rules to disambiguate borderline cases. We also feed cases where GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-Pro disagree on the test set back into the refinement loop as additional signals. Finally, we finalize 23 distinct subcategories. After the category set is fixed, we continue refining and adding boundary rules to reduce model confusion and produce the final prompt for classification.

1https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2025/ReviewerGuidelines

2https://aclrollingreview.org/reviewform

表 4.Table 4.CoCoReviewBench 类别和子类别的完整分类。Complete taxonomy of CoCoReviewBench categories and subcategories.

类别Category 子类别和描述Subcategories and Descriptions
品质Quality 质量方法Quality-Method（方法论的健全性）：评估所提出的算法、模型或系统是否(Methodological Soundness): Evaluates whether the proposed algorithms, models, or system
架构在技术上是正确的，并且没有概念或实现错误。architectures are technically correct and free of conceptual or implementation errors.
质量实验Quality-Experiment（实验设计与评估）：评估实验设置的充分性：(Experimental Design & Evaluation): Assesses the adequacy of the experimental setup:
数据集、基线、评估指标、消融研究和统计测试的选择。choice of datasets, baselines, evaluation metrics, ablation studies, and statistical tests.
质量-再现性Quality-Reproducibility（再现性和实施细节）：考虑提交的内容是否提供(Reproducibility & Implementation Details): Considers whether the submission provides
足够的细节（例如，超参数、代码可用性、数据集分割）来复制工作以及是否enough detail (e.g., hyperparameters, code availability, dataset splits) to replicate the work and whether the
实施遵循最佳实践。implementation follows best practices.
质量比较Quality-Comparisons（与之前的工作比较）：评估论文是否与之前的工作进行了充分的比较(Comparisons to Prior Work): Evaluates whether the paper suffciently compares against
相关的先前工作和最先进的方法。relevant prior work and state-of-the-art methods.
质量统计Quality-Statistics（统计严谨性和验证）：评估统计分析（例如显着性检验、(Statistical Rigor & Validation): Evaluates whether statistical analyses (e.g., signifcance tests,
置信区间）是否得到正确应用，以及报告的改进是否具有统计意义。confdence intervals) are properly applied and whether reported improvements are statistically meaningful.
清晰度Clarity 清晰的写作Clarity-Writing（写作、术语和算法演示）：涵盖整体写作质量和组织(Writing, Terminology & Algorithm Presentation): Covers overall writing quality and orga-
术语和关键概念的标准化、准确性以及算法演示的清晰度（代码片段、nization, precision of terminology and key concepts, and the clarity of algorithm presentations (code snippets,
伪代码、工作流程图）。pseudocode, workfow diagrams).
清晰度符号Clarity-Notation（符号和数学解释清晰度）：考虑数学符号是否正确(Notation & Mathematical Explanation Clarity): Considers whether mathematical notation is
一致、明确定义和解释。consistent, well defned and explained.
清晰度-图/表Clarity-Figure/Table（图形和视觉辅助清晰度）：评估是否使用图形、表格、绘图和可视化(Figures & Visual Aids Clarity): Evaluates whether fgures, tables, plots, and visualizations
清晰易读、正确标记并有助于理解。are legible, properly labeled, and aid understanding.
意义Signifcance 广泛意义Signifcance-Broad（广泛的研究影响）：评估该工作是否解决了广泛重要的问题(Broad Research Impact): Evaluates whether the work addresses a broadly important problem
或以可能影响多个研究领域的方式增进理解。or advances understanding in a way that is likely to infuence multiple research areas.
意义域Signifcance-Domain（领域/应用影响）：评估特定贡献的重要性(Domain/Applied Impact): Assesses the signifcance of the contribution for a specifc
应用领域（例如 NLP、机器人、医疗保健）。application domain (e.g., NLP, robotics, healthcare).
意义-最新技术Signifcance-StateOfTheArt（对现有技术的改进）：重点关注(Improvement Over State of the Art): Focuses on the magnitude and importance of
相对于当前最先进的方法的改进。improvements relative to current state-of-the-art methods.
意义-影响Signifcance-Impact（现实世界和社会影响）：考虑潜在的实际和社会影响(Real-World & Societal Impact): Considers the potential practical and societal ramifcations
工作的内容，包括收益、风险、公平性、环境影响和可及性。of the work, including benefts, risks, fairness, environmental impacts and accessibility.
原创性Originality 原创性问题Originality-Problem（新问题表述）：对引入新问题、任务或新问题的评论(Novel Problem Formulation): Comments on the introduction of a new problem, task, or
数据集。包括突出以前未解决的挑战的创新问题定义。dataset. Includes innovative problem defnitions that highlight previously unaddressed challenges.
独创性-方法Originality-Method（新颖的方法论或算法）：评估论文是否引入了真正的新方法(Novel Methodology or Algorithm): Assesses whether the paper introduces a genuinely new
算法方法或架构设计，而不是对现有方法的微小调整。algorithmic approach or architectural design rather than a minor tweak of existing methods.
原创性分析Originality-Analysis（新颖的分析或见解）：涉及深入的原创理论见解或分析(Novel Analysis or Insights): Pertains to original theoretical insights or analyses that deepen
对现有方法或现象的理解。understanding of existing methods or phenomena.
原创性实验Originality-Experiment（新颖的实验设置或数据）：评估论文是否提出了新的实验(Novel Experimental Setup or Data): Evaluates whether the paper proposes new experi-
心理设置、基准、评估协议或收集新的数据集。mental setups, benchmarks, evaluation protocols or collects new datasets.
创意-组合Originality-Combination（现有方法的创造性组合）：考虑论文是否结合了(Creative Combination of Existing Methods): Considers whether the paper combines
以原创的方式了解众所周知的技术，以及组合背后的基本原理是否令人信服。well-known techniques in an original way and whether the rationale behind the combination is compelling.
原创性-负面结果Originality-NegativeResults（负面结果或批判性评估）：涵盖提供批判性结果的论文(Negative Results or Critical Assessments): Covers papers that provide critical
评估、消融或负面结果表明现有方法的局限性。evaluations, ablations or negative results showing limitations of existing methods.
政策Policy 政策伦理Policy-Ethics（道德与负责任的人工智能合规性）：解决道德问题，包括公平、偏见、伤害(Ethics & Responsible AI Compliance): Addresses ethical concerns, including fairness, bias, harms
对边缘化人群、双重用途以及作者是否适当讨论和减轻道德风险。to marginalized populations, dual use and whether the authors appropriately discuss and mitigate ethical risks.
政策-数据隐私Policy-DataPrivacy（数据使用和隐私合规性）：考虑论文中使用的数据是否合规(Data Usage & Privacy Compliance): Considers whether the data used in the paper comply
遵守隐私法规和许可条款（例如同意、个人身份信息）。with privacy regulations and licensing terms (e.g., consent, personally identifable information).
政策匿名Policy-Anonymity（双盲或匿名违规）：关注是否无意中提交(Double-Blind or Anonymity Violations): Concerns whether the submission inadvertently
泄露作者身份或机构隶属关系，违反双盲审查政策。reveals author identities or institutional affliations, violating double-blind review policies.
政策剽窃Policy-Plagiarism（抄袭和双重提交）：解决抄袭、自我抄袭或双重提交问题(Plagiarism & Dual Submission): Addresses issues of plagiarism, self-plagiarism, or dual
提交到多个地点。submission to multiple venues.
政策更广泛的影响Policy-BroaderImpact（更广泛的影响和社会考虑）：评估作者是否完成(Broader Impact & Societal Considerations): Evaluates whether the authors complete
所需的清单并讨论他们的工作对社会的更广泛影响。required checklists and discuss the broader impact of their work on society.

B.1.2。哪些审查维度与最终决定最一致B.1.2. WHICH REVIEW DIMENSIONS MOST ALIGN WITH FINAL DECISIONS

单个审稿人的覆盖范围不完整。Incomplete Coverage for a Single Reviewer.完整结果如表 5 所示。覆盖率列（单 Cov. 与所有 Cov.）显示，单个审稿人并不能始终涵盖所有核心方面：不到一半的个人审稿 (47.55%) 中提到了重要性，这对于主要评估标准来说出乎意料地低。政策也很少被提及，这更容易被接受，因为政策问题通常只在少数论文中触发。汇总每篇论文的所有审稿人大大增加了覆盖范围（例如，重要性上升到 83.44%），但仍然留下 16.56% 的论文没有任何明确的重要性讨论，表明汇总有所帮助，但仍不足以保证完整性。The full results are shown in Table 5. The coverage columns (Single Cov. vs. All Cov.) show that a single reviewer does not consistently cover all core aspects: Significance is mentioned in fewer than half of individual reviews (47.55%), which is unexpectedly low for a primary evaluation criterion. Policy is also rarely mentioned, which is more acceptable because policy issues are typically triggered only in a small minority of papers. Aggregating all reviewers per paper substantially increases coverage (e.g., Significance rises to 83.44%), but still leaves 16.56% of papers without any explicit Significance discussion, indicating that aggregation helps yet remains insufficient for completeness.

The largest associations are Originality-Method, Quality-Experiment, OriginalityAnalysis, and Significance-StateOfTheArt, followed by Clarity-Writing and Quality-Comparisons. 哪里wherex ˜ 我,·i,·是上面定义的平均估算子类别分数。总体而言，对总体得分影响最大的类别在这些互补指标中保持一致，强调了它们的重要性。are the mean-imputed subcategory scores as defined above. Overall, the categories with the largest influence on the overall score remain consistent across these complementary metrics, underscoring their importance.

缺失效应表明缺席并不意味着中立。Missingness Effects Show that Absence is not Neutrality.提及与缺失的比较表明，某些类别主要是在负面背景下提出的。最大的负面变化发生在政策剽窃和政策匿名方面。尽管这些事件很少发生，但它们表明与政策相关的担忧可能会严重压低预期的总体得分。相比之下，原创性-负面结果和重要性-影响力在存在时表现出积极的转变，表明明确表达的新颖性和影响力陈述伴随着更高的分数。这警告不要将“未提及”视为稀疏评论中隐含的负面标签。Mentioned-vs-missing comparisons reveal that certain categories are raised mainly in negative contexts. The largest negative shifts occur for Policy-Plagiarism and PolicyAnonymity. Although these are rare events, they indicate that policy-related concerns can substantially depress the expected overall score. In contrast, Originality-NegativeResults and Significance-Impact show positive shifts when present, suggesting that explicitly articulated novelty and impact statements accompany higher scores. This cautions against treating “not mentioned” as an implicit negative label in sparse reviews.

复合信号可以很好地预测高分，但低分仍然很难预测。Composite Signals Predict High Scores Well, but Low Scores Remain Hard.使用最多四个维度的综合得分搜索，最佳“HIGH”分类器Using the composite-score search over up to four dimensions, the best “HIGH” classifier fory ≥6 实现F1=06 achieves F1= 0.7827（P=07827 (P= 0.6870，R=06870, R= 0.第9092章）9092) atτ =1 .6803 的指标包括清晰度写作、原创性方法、质量比较和质量实验。表现最好的集合高度一致，并且反复包含由相关性和岭确定的相同核心维度。相反，预测“低”（6803 with metrics Clarity-Writing, Originality-Method, Quality-Comparisons, and Quality-Experiment. B.1.3。不完整的人类参考类别的评估偏差B.1.3. EVALUATION BIAS FROM INCOMPLETE HUMAN REFERENCE CATEGORIES

我们进一步测试当人类评论直接用作评估人工智能评论者的参考时，不完整的人类类别覆盖是否会引入偏差。对于每篇论文，我们根据人类参考文献涵盖的类别将生成的人工智能评论分为两个不相交的子集：We further test whether incomplete human category coverage introduces bias when human reviews are directly used as references for evaluating AI reviewers. For each paper, we split the generated AI review into two disjoint subsets according to the categories covered by the human references:类别一致category-consistent意见，其类别由人类参考涵盖，以及opinions, whose categories are covered by the human reference, and类别不一致category-inconsistent意见，其类别在人类参考中不存在。然后，我们使用与第 5.1 节中相同的论文级 LLM 作为法官协议，根据相同的论文级人类参考分别评估两个子集。我们只保留两个子集都非空且长度相当的论文，要求它们的字符长度比在 2x 以内。负增量意味着类别不一致子集的得分低于类别一致子集的得分。opinions, whose categories are absent from the human reference. We then evaluate the two subsets separately against the same paper-level human reference, using the same paper-level LLM-as-a-judge protocol as in Section 5.1. We keep only papers where both subsets are non-empty and have comparable length, requiring their character-length ratio to be within 2x. A negative delta means that the category-inconsistent subset receives a lower score than the category-consistent subset.

如表 6 所示，所有四个模型中类别不一致的意见得分始终较低。最大的下降出现在正确性和彻底性方面，这最直接取决于与参考的一致性，而基础性、可验证性和清晰度方面的下降较小。这表明，绩效差距不仅是由于总体评论质量较低造成的，而且还反映了系统性评估偏差：一旦将人类评论视为参考宇宙，人类参考中缺失的类别的有效评论可能会处于不利地位。此外，即使汇总了所有审稿人，人工审稿平均也仅涵盖 23 个子类别中的 9.23 个。它表明，将人类提及的内容视为完整的参考可以创建稀疏且有偏差的评估目标。As shown in Table 6, category-inconsistent opinions are consistently scored lower across all four models. The largest drops appear in Correctness and Thoroughness, which depend most directly on agreement with the reference, while the drops in Grounding, Verifiability, and Clarity are smaller. This suggests that the performance gap is not solely caused by lower general review quality, but also reflects a systematic evaluation bias: once human reviews are treated as the reference universe, valid comments from categories missing in the human reference can be disadvantaged. Further, even after aggregating all reviewers, human reviews cover only 9.23 out of 23 subcategories on average. It shows that treating human-mentioned content as a complete reference can create a sparse and biased evaluation target.

表 6.Table 6.在论文级评估下，对类别不一致和类别一致的人工智能意见之间的差异进行评分。负值表示来自人类参考中缺少的类别的意见得分较低。Score differences between category-inconsistent and category-consistent AI opinions under paper-level evaluation. Negative values indicate that opinions from categories absent in human references are scored lower.

B.2.审稿错误的类别分布B.2. Category Distribution of Reviewer Incorrectness

我们进一步分析审稿人在细粒度分类中出现错误的位置。我们将基准中识别出的每个不正确意见分配给其中一个子类别，并报告包含该类别中至少一个不正确意见的论文的百分比。结果如图 6 所示。We further analyze where reviewer incorrectness occurs in our fine-grained taxonomy. We assign each identified incorrect opinion in our benchmark to one of the subcategories, and report the percentage of papers that contain at least one incorrect opinion of that category. The results are shown in Figure 6.

图 6.Figure 6.审稿人错误的子类别分布。Subcategory distribution of reviewer incorrectness.左：Left:审稿人之间的冲突。inter-reviewer conflicts.右：Right:审稿人与作者之间的冲突。颜色表示相应的主要类别。reviewer–author conflicts. Colors indicate the corresponding major categories.

审稿人之间的冲突。Inter-Reviewer Conflicts.审稿人之间最大的差距集中在原创性方法、清晰度写作和质量实验，其次是质量方法和原创性分析。这表明审稿人之间的实质性分歧相对普遍，并且可能出现在许多不同的类别中。The largest inter-reviewer gaps concentrate on Originality-Method, Clarity-Writing, and Quality-Experiment, followed by Quality-Method and Originality-Analysis. This indicates that substantial inter-reviewer disagreements are relatively widespread and can arise across many different categories.

审稿人与作者之间的冲突。Reviewer-Author Conflicts.审稿人与作者之间的冲突主要是与质量相关的子类别，特别是质量实验、质量方法和质量比较。我们还观察到写作清晰度和原创性方法之间存在显着冲突。总体而言，作者反驳最常在具体的技术细节（例如实验设置、方法假设和比较的充分性）上向审稿人提出质疑，这表明审稿人的错误很大一部分源于论文的正确性。Reviewer-author conflicts are dominated by Quality-related subcategories, especially Quality-Experiment, Quality-Method, and Quality-Comparisons. We also observe notable conflicts on Clarity-Writing and Originality-Method. Overall, author rebuttals most often challenge reviewers on concrete technical details such as experimental setups, methodological assumptions, and the adequacy of comparisons, suggesting that a large portion of reviewer errors stem from the correctness of the paper.

21

CoCoReviewBench：面向人工智能审阅者的完整性和正确性基准CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers

B.3. CoCoReviewBench 的步骤级验证和模型选择B.3. Step-Level Verification and Model Selection for CoCoReviewBench

我们的CoCoReviewBench构建过程分为多个步骤。为了验证每个步骤的质量，并为 CoCoReviewBench 构建工作流程的每个阶段选择最合适的 LLM，我们进行了一项初步研究，衡量跨模型一致性，并使用它来为我们的流程选择性能最佳的模型。通过相互验证，我们解决了缺乏黄金参考的问题，从而实现更可靠的验证。Our CoCoReviewBench construction process is divided into multiple steps. To verify the quality of each step, and select the most suitable LLM for each stage of the CoCoReviewBench construction workflow, we conduct a preliminary study that measures cross-model consistency and uses it to select the best-performing models for our pipeline. Through mutual verification, we address the lack of a gold reference, enabling more reliable validation.

具体来说，我们的工作流程涉及八次法学硕士通话。首先，在§4.2.1中，我们将每个评论分割成原子意见（评论分割），然后对原子意见进行分类（评论分类），并进一步分割包含多个类别的原子意见（二次分割），总共三个LLM调用。然后，在§4.2.2中，我们对讨论同一主题的评论进行聚类（评论聚类），然后分别检查审稿人之间（审稿人间）以及审稿人和作者之间（审稿人-作者）之间的冲突。每次检查由两个步骤组成：首先识别评论组内是否存在冲突（Conflicts-Identification），然后根据元审查，确定冲突组中哪些意见是正确或不正确的（Conflicts-Resolution），总共五次LLM调用。Specifically, our workflow involves eight LLM calls. First, in §4.2.1, we segment each review into atomic opinions (Review Segmentation), then classify the atomic opinions (Review Classification), and further split atomic opinions that contain multiple categories (Secondary Segmentation), totaling three LLM calls. Then, in §4.2.2, we cluster comments that discuss the same topic (Review Clustering) and then separately examine conflicts between reviewers (Inter-Reviewer) and between reviewers and authors (Reviewer-Author). Each examination consists of two steps: first identifying whether a conflict exists within a comment group (Conflicts-Identification), and then, based on the meta-review, determining which opinions in the conflicting group are correct or incorrect (Conflicts-Resolution), totaling five LLM calls.

B.3.1。实验装置B.3.1. EXPERIMENTAL SETUP

候选模型。Candidate Models.我们评估了六种领先的法学硕士：GPT-5、GPT-5-Mini、Gemini-2.5-Pro、Gemini-2.5-Flash、DeepSeekR1（Guo 等人，2025）和 DeepSeek-V3（Liu 等人，2024a），它们涵盖了广泛的成本和性能概况。We evaluate six leading LLMs: GPT-5, GPT-5-Mini, Gemini-2.5-Pro, Gemini-2.5-Flash, DeepSeekR1 (Guo et al., 2025), and DeepSeek-V3 (Liu et al., 2024a), which span a wide range of cost and performance profiles.

留一交叉验证。Leave-One-Out Cross-Validation.我们对 180 篇未包含在我们的基准中的 2020 年后论文进行了抽样，每年 20 篇论文作为验证集。对于每个管道步骤，我们在相同的输入上运行所有六个模型，这些模型是由上一步中选择的性能最佳模型的输出生成的。由于这些任务不存在黄金答案，因此我们遵循 Weng 等人的观点。 （2025）并使用留一法交叉验证策略：对于每个输入，我们将从其他五个模型得出的共识视为伪地面事实，并通过将保留模型的输出与该共识进行比较来计算一致性分数。然后，我们对每个步骤的候选模型进行排名，以选择最佳模型。We sample 180 post-2020 papers that are not included in our benchmark, with 20 papers per year serving as a validation set. For each pipeline step, we run all six models on the same inputs, which are generated by the outputs of the best-performing model selected in the previous step. Because no gold answers exist for these tasks, we follow Weng et al. (2025) and use a leave-one-out cross-validation strategy: for each input, we treat the consensus derived from the other five models as a pseudo-ground truth and compute a consistency score by comparing the held-out model’s output against this consensus. We then rank candidate models for each step to select the best model.

指标。Metrics.对于集成多个组件的任务，包括评论细分和评论聚类，我们通过计算成对的模型来评估跨模型一致性For tasks that integrate multiple components, including Review Segmentation and Review Clustering, we assess cross-model agreement by computing the pairwise欧米茄指数Omega Index模型之间。对于多类分类任务，包括评论分类和二级分割，我们测量between models. For multi-class classification tasks, including Review Classification and Secondary Segmentation, we measure一致性Consistency通过 (i) 产生完全相同标签的模型对的比例和 (ii) 产生部分相同标签集的比例的平均值。由于硬投票在上述设置中通常不可靠，因此我们使用软标签方案：对于每个实例，我们将其他五个模型的平均预测视为软标签，并计算每个模型与该软标签的一致性，并通过模型对之间的平均一致性来计算。对于二元分类任务，包括审稿人间冲突识别和解决以及审稿人与作者冲突识别和解决，我们改为计算by the average of (i) the proportion of model pairs that produce exactly identical labels and (ii) the proportion that produce partially identical label sets. Because hard voting is often unreliable in the above setting, we use a soft-label scheme: for each instance, we treat the mean prediction of the other five models as a soft label and compute each model’s agreement with this soft label, and we calculate this by the average agreement across model pairs. For binary classification tasks, including Inter-Reviewer Conflict Identification and Resolution and Reviewer-Author Conflict Identification and Resolution, we instead compute theF1成绩F1 score使用多数票伪金标签：六个模型中至少有四个选择的选项作为金标签，如果平局（六分之三），我们分配 0.5 分。所有型号均保留这些金色标签。using a majority-vote pseudo-gold label: an option selected by at least four of the six models serves as the gold label, and we assign a score of 0.5 in the event of a tie (3 out of 6). These gold labels are preserved across all models.

B.3.2。评选结果B.3.2. SELECTION RESULTS

表 7 列出了评论细分、评论分类、二级细分和评论聚类的交叉验证结果。对于评论细分，GPT-5 的卓越性能确保了它的选择。在审查分类中，在平衡 GPT-5 和 Gemini-2.5-Pro 的类似功能与成本考虑后，选择了 GPT-5-Mini。 GPT-5-Mini 在二次分割方面也表现出色，因此也成为此步骤的指定模型。最后，DeepSeekR1 在回顾聚类中的最佳性能决定了它在该任务中的采用。Table 7 presents the cross-validation results for Review Segmentation, Review Classification, Secondary Segmentation and Review Clustering. For Review Segmentation, GPT-5’s superior performance secures its selection. In Review Classification, GPT-5-Mini is chosen after balancing comparable capabilities from GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-Pro against cost considerations. GPT-5-Mini also excels in Secondary Segmentation, making it the assigned model for this step as well. Finally, DeepSeekR1’s optimal performance in Review Clustering determines its adoption for that task.

如表 8 所示，对于审阅者间冲突识别和解决，DeepSeek-R1 提供了最佳性能，用于此步骤。对于Reviewer-Author冲突识别，为了平衡性能和成本，我们计算了Gemini-2.5-Flash和GPT-5-Mini判断交集的一致性，达到了87.67的一致性值，以较低的成本优于GPT-5的84.56。对于审阅者与作者冲突解决，DeepSeek-R1 显示出最佳性能并用于此步骤。总体而言，上述结果显示了不同模型之间的高度一致性，证明了我们设计的稳健性和有效性。As shown in Table 8, for Inter-Reviewer Conflict Identification and Resolution, DeepSeek-R1 delivers the best performance, which is used for this step. For Reviewer-Author Conflict Identification, to balance performance and cost, we calculate the consistency of the intersection between Gemini-2.5-Flash and GPT-5-Mini judgments, achieving a consistency value of 87.67, which outperforms GPT-5’s 84.56 at a lower cost. For Reviewer-Author Conflict Resolution, DeepSeek-R1 shows the best performance and is used for this step. Overall, the results above show high consistency across different models, demonstrating the robustness and effectiveness of our design.

根据这些结果，我们为每个特定子任务使用性能最佳的模型选择混合管道，将 3,900 篇论文的总构建成本保持在约 1,000 美元，同时最大限度地提高可靠性。Based on these results, we select a hybrid pipeline using the best-performing model for each specific sub-task, keeping the total construction cost to approximately $1,000 for 3,900 papers while maximizing reliability.
